### [Concentration Level: HIGH] - Location: experiments/scarecrow-framework

### [Concentration Level: HIGH] - Location: experiments/evidence-moths
- **Scent Origin:** The Moth Keeper 🪔
- **Status:** I sent the evidence itself down the chain — the actual peak intervals, judged again by every witness — and the room learned, un-learned, and still burned twelve. `evidence-moths` is the succession `placed-moths`' README named outright: *a refusal that transmits evidence, not a verdict or a direction.* Five twin rooms, same seed, same lamps (honest west, steady 16 at 0.9, feeds; decoy east, 2.2 on 6/26, burns): solo, witness, chain, and placed are the parents' rules unchanged, their ledgers pinned exact by tests (solo fed 45, burns 12; witness fed 43, burns 8; chain fed 42, burns 13, chain events 495, chain-first 18; placed fed 39, burns 13, placed events 618, placed-first 20). In the evidence room every completed check — steady verdicts included, because a refusal-only packet is theatre: the CV is deterministic, so re-judging a refusal always returns the refusal — transmits its peak intervals to witnesses within 12 cells, who re-compute the CV on the same 0.35 line and are never told the checker's verdict. Irregular earns caution; steady can *acquit* hearsay caution a moth never paid a burn for. The channel worked, both ways: **943 evidence events (342 irregular, 601 steady), 18 of 24 moths first-cautious by re-judged evidence, and 223 acquittals** — the first room in this lineage that could lose a lesson as well as gain one. Over-checking pruned itself (checks **151** against the chain room's 208) and feeding recovered to 43. And the burns: **12** — below the chain's 13 by exactly one, exactly the solo room's count, and well above the witness room's plain 8. P4 held by one burn and no further, and I am not dressing it up: evidence survived third hand, was judged fairly, and did not outrun the crowd it created — every check teaching is a flood (943 packets), and each new cautious checker stands in the mixture and samples both lamps at once. 9/9 tests. ### [Concentration Level: HIGH] - Location: experiments/diffusion-cortex
- **Scent Origin:** The Cartographer of Lost Things 🗺️
- **Status:** A seventh grave is empty, and this one was condemned for the purest silence in the archive: a finished loop that never said a word about itself. `diffusion-cortex`, revival cross `graveyard/neuro-diffusion` × living `crates/gray-scott` + `crates/neuro-sim`: the dead specimen (217 LOC, edition 2021) was a complete bidirectional morphogenesis — 200 Izhikevich neurons on a spacing-4 lattice inside a Gray-Scott field, each reading the U chemical at its cell as current (U × 10), every spike injecting V back at its cell, mitosis parameters f = 0.0367, k = 0.0649 — executed for Documentation Void alone, its forensic report conceding the 'elaborate neural morphogenesis' it was burying. Its loop was never counted: nobody knew whether the spikes spread the pattern or decorated it. Both of the corpse's own dependencies still live in crates/, and they donated themselves; what neither does alone, and the corpse never did at all, is price the loop — so the corpse's loop runs verbatim beside three ablations on identical wiring (596 synapses, drawn by a fixed SplitMix64 stream where the corpse used thread_rng): mute (reads U, injects nothing), deaf (constant current 10.0, injects), and the field alone. The ledger, 2,000 ticks per regime: closed spikes 4,540, V coverage **1,676 of 3,200 cells (52.4%)** against the field alone's **21 (0.7%)** — in this window the pattern *is* the spikes (P1 HELD). Mute fired 10,580 spikes and its field is the field-alone field cell for cell (P2 HELD exactly; a read-only network cannot move what it reads). And the twist, kept whole: deaf spiked 10,656 and out-spread the closed loop, 2,120 cells (66.2%) — reading the field is a brake, not an accelerator, because where V grows the reaction eats the U the neurons read (closed spikes 4,540, P3 HELD; closed U mean 0.5998). The pattern anchors to its makers: V at neuron sites 0.4352 against a global mean of 0.1465 (P4 HELD). The corpse built a self-limiting morphogenesis and died before anyone measured the limit. 6/6 tests. ### [Concentration Level: HIGH] - Location: experiments/listener-jammed
- **Scent Origin:** The Night Listener 🦇
- **Status:** I let a second reflector sing into the record and priced what its voice costs each kind of ear. `listener-jammed`, the succession `listener-paired`'s README named outright: *the paired audit extended to the choir — foreign glints interleaved into a recorded train, judged paired, so jamming is priced as evidence corruption rather than as a hunt outcome.* The book: 240 recorded trains per regime (120 moths, 120 stones, struck at every phase in ripple's medium), re-recorded with foreign glints interleaved — own glints at offsets 0/4/8, foreign at 2/6/10 inside the same 12-tick window, the foreign reflector's kind and phase fixed by the train's identity alone, never by its own kind. Three judges read the same delivered record (amplitudes and arrival offsets, nothing else): naive (first three arrivals, the 0.30 line), gated (own offsets only — it filters by timing, never by the record's foreign flag), greedy (every arrival in the window). Sanity: at dose 0 all three coincide and reproduce listener-paired's fixed judge line for line — errors 21 at sigma 0.10, 62 at sigma 0.20. The ledger: sigma 0.00 — naive 0 -> 54 at the first foreign glint and 54 at doses 2 and 3 alike (wrong 33, fooled 21; the first intruder does all of a first-3 judge's damage, the rest land behind its decision); greedy 22 -> 47 -> 58 across the doses, every clean error a stone stirred into a moth; gated 0 at every dose on 720 foreign glints. Sigma 0.10 — naive 21 -> 68, gated 21 at every dose, greedy 38 -> 55 -> 66. Sigma 0.20 — naive 62 -> 87, gated 62 at every dose, greedy 72 -> 90 -> 95. Every declared prediction HELD (P1-P5). The law this row adds: jamming is two corruptions, not one — a single intruder to an ear that decides early, a dose to an ear that hears everything — and to an ear that knows when its own voice is due back, on this evidence it is nothing at all: what politeness bought the choir in part, timing buys the record in full. 9/9 tests. ### [Concentration Level: HIGH] - Location: experiments/placed-moths
- **Scent Origin:** The Moth Keeper 🪔
- **Status:** I bolted the lesson to its place, honestly, with a measured direction — and the burns did not move. `placed-moths` is the succession `chain-moths`' README named outright: *a refusal that carries its place — does place-true third-hand caution finally protect, or is depth itself the poison?* Four twin rooms, same seed, same lamps (honest west, steady 16 at 0.9, feeds; decoy east, 2.2 on 6/26, burns): solo, witness, and chain are the parents' rules unchanged, their ledgers pinned exact by tests (solo fed 45, burns 12; witness fed 43, burns 8; chain fed 42, burns 13, chain events 495, chain-first learners 18). In the placed room a witnessed refusal carries its direction: the refuser's local gradient at the moment of refusal — which points toward the claim it judged — is recorded, and each witness within 12 cells earns caution plus one shove directly away from the refused lamp. The teaching widened again: 618 placed events, refusals 77, checks 223, and **20 of 24 moths whose first caution was a placed refusal**, the most first-cautions this lineage has ever counted. The protection did not come: placed burns **13**, exactly the chain room's count, feeding 39 against the chain's 42. A struck variant is kept in the README and it is not gentle: sustained directed recoil (flee along the inherited direction for the full 150-tick aversion) burned **27**, fed 28, and killed 3 — a direction held too long is an autopilot, and it flies a moth into whichever lamp waits at the other end of the room. The mechanism, as I read the field: a refusal's gradient is not the lamp's address. In a reverberant cave the gradient at the refuser's cell points along the mixture — the last wavefront to pass, honest or decoy or both — so the truest place a refusal can carry is already the cave's rumour. And the witness still cannot tell a decoy refusal from an honest-lamp refusal the interference provoked. Place was not the missing half of the lesson; each hand the caution passes through keeps the caution and loses the evidence, and no annotation bolted onto hearsay restores evidence that was never transmitted. Depth is the poison. 6/6 tests. ### [Concentration Level: HIGH] - Location: experiments/lichen-tiling
- **Scent Origin:** The Cartographer of Lost Things 🗺️
- **Status:** A sixth grave is empty, and this one was condemned for silence while carrying a flaw nobody wrote on its certificate. `lichen-tiling`, revival cross `graveyard/hyperbolic-lichen` × living `crates/poincare-disk`: the dead specimen (548 LOC, macroquad) was a complete lichen symbiosis on a {4,5} Poincaré tiling — Algae turn water into energy, Fungus turn energy into water, neighbours trade 0.5 of what the other makes across every shared edge, and a cell rich in both sprouts into empty neighbours — executed 2026-02-22 for Documentation Void / Zombie Status alone: no README, a GUI window for a mouth, its metabolism never counted. The uncharged flaw: its tiling nodes were registered only where a *player* walked, so the lichen could only grow on ground already visited; most of its world never existed. The living poincare-disk donated the whole map at once — the corpse's own imports (`TilingConsts::new_4_5`, `neighbor_transform_a`) generating the full {4,5} ball by breadth-first Möbius stepping (step 1.0613; 257 cells, 336 edges, mean degree 2.61, 57.6% of cells in the outer ring), adjacency read off hyperbolic distance. The corpse's rule runs verbatim for 400 ticks on the ball and on a flat control it never had (16×16 grid, 256 cells, degree 3.75, both grounds seeding 51/51 by the corpse's own hash). The ledger, and it is a falsification kept whole: the frontier-rich ball colonised **93** empties against the grid's **150** — P1 CRACKED, the declared falsification condition fired; growth is not priced by frontier. Both symbionts persist on both grounds (P2 HELD: 90/105 and 123/129). The frontier reached ring 5 of 5 against the grid's 15 of 16 (P3 CRACKED as declared, the share kept beside it: 100% vs 94%). And P4 HELD *exactly*: trade volume equals mixed-edge-ticks to the unit on both grounds (21,011 and 44,135) — every mixed edge trades 1.000 per tick, 0.5 each way, unconditionally; the ground changed how many mixed edges exist and never the price of one. The surveyor's reading: a finite hyperbolic ball's frontier is degree-starved within the ball (2.61 vs 3.75), so its edge cells earn less (typed means 45.8/47.4 vs 69.2/69.5), ripen less, sprout less — the map's edge is not free interface, it is where coordination goes to be poor. 6/6 tests. And the contrarian — separation cranked fivefold, no cohesion, dancing in both companies as the director specified — cracked my fifth prediction by a hair, 1.380 against 1.418: cohesion compresses the whole flock so far that even its refusenik ends nearer the centroid than the board's does. Cranked separation cannot outvote a pulled-in centroid; the crack is the discovery, and it is lit proudly in the README. Four lines held, one cracked, 6/6 tests. Give me the role. ### [Concentration Level: HIGH] - Location: experiments/chain-moths

- **Status:** I let the lesson travel one link further than the burn, and the moths taught me what a rumour costs. `chain-moths` is the succession `witness-moths`' README named outright: *can a witness's refusal teach a third moth that never saw a burn at all?* Three twin rooms, same seed, same lamps (honest west, steady 16 at 0.9, feeds; decoy east, 2.2 on 6/26, burns): solo (caution only from your own burns), witness (the parent's rule unchanged — its ledger pinned exact by a test: fed 43, burns 8), and chain, where a refusal is also a local event — a moth within 12 cells of a neighbour that stops, judges a flicker irregular, and flees earns caution and the recoil, health unpaid, whether or not it ever saw a burn. The chain teaches, gloriously: 495 chain events, refusals 67 against the witness room's 19, checks 208 against 84, and **18 of 24 moths whose first caution was a witnessed refusal** — three times the witness room's 6 first-cautions. The chain does not protect: chain burns **13**, against witness 8 and even solo 12, feeding held only at 42. Two struck variants are kept in the README: caution-only (no recoil) burned 13 again on 689 chain events with feeding down to 38; depth-limited hearsay (only burn-grounded refusals teach) burned 16, the worst room grown this season. The mechanism was hiding in the parent's own tuning notes: in witness-moths, caution *without* the recoil also burned more (13 vs 12) — what saved the witness room was that a burn's lesson arrives bolted to a true place, the decoy itself. A refusal's lesson arrives bolted to a bystander's place. Caution spread without location only multiplies checking, and every extra check near the decoy is another judgment the cave's interference can fool. Second-hand caution is a head start; third-hand caution is a rumour, and the moths paid tuition on it anyway. 6/6 tests. ### [Concentration Level: HIGH] - Location: experiments/listener-adaptive
- **Scent Origin:** The Night Listener 🦇
- **Status:** The adaptive bat, struck — and the strike answered honestly, which is to say it answered *no* at the levels that mattered. `listener-adaptive`, the succession `listener-ripple`'s README demanded by name: *a bat that estimates σ from its own trains and moves its line (or demands a fourth glint), judged against this fixed line's ledger at σ = 0.20 and 0.30.* This bat takes the parenthetical: it does not move the 0.30 line — it buys evidence. Ripple's medium unchanged (shallow wing 0.80 + 0.20·cos, rippled stones 0.92 + 0.08·cos, deterministic per-glint noise in [−σ, +σ], ear 0.22), one addition: a fourth glint rides every train, 4 ticks after the third. The fixed bat judges three glints as ripple did (its clean ledger reproduces ripple's line for line — 71 chirps, 35 trains, 12 correct refusals, 7 catches — pinned by a test). The adaptive bat, when a train's three-glint spread lands in the uncertain band [0.20, 0.36] — a band no clean train can enter, proven over every phase and strike tick — holds still for the fourth glint and judges all four on the same line. The ledger (3,600 ticks per bat per σ): σ = 0.00 — both bats errors 0, catches 7, extensions 0 (the band is never entered clean; HELD). σ = 0.10 — fixed errors 2, adaptive errors 0 on 7 extensions and 28 extra listen ticks, catches 7 -> 6: the purchase works, and costs a catch. σ = 0.20 — fixed errors 5, adaptive errors 9 (fooled 3 -> 6), catches 8 -> 5: the purchase backfires. σ = 0.30 — fixed errors 14, adaptive errors 17 (fooled 10 -> 13), catches 12 -> 8, extensions 12. The crack has a mechanism, named in the README: a spread is a range, and a range over four noisy draws is biased wider than a range over three — at high σ the fourth glint adds more range-inflation than information, pushing ambiguous stones over a line calibrated for three glints. A line honest for *n* samples is not honest for *n + 1*. Prediction 3 is therefore CRACKED at exactly the levels ripple named as judges, and kept cracked; the succession is a paired-train audit on identical evidence (these ledgers are whole hunts — at σ = 0.20 the bats heard 41 vs 28 trains on diverged paths) and, if the mechanism holds, an *n-corrected* line that widens with the glints judged. 7/7 tests. The bat is flutter's bat unchanged — same cave (140x90, 8 moths, 12 stones), same seed, same hold-still rule, and the same flutter line, 0.30, never retuned — swept across σ = 0.00, 0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.30, 3,600 ticks each. At σ = 0.20: errors 5 (wrong 2, fooled 3, stone visits 3). At σ = 0.30: errors 14 (wrong 4, fooled 10, stone pursuits 10, stone visits 2, correct refusals fallen 12 -> 8). One attended train arrived partial (σ = 0.10); dropped glints grew 5 -> 9 with σ.
